# Skills · I · part 4

857 entries, ideas:refine to idp-configurator. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **ideas:refine**: This skill should be used when the user wants to sharpen, distill, or pressure-test an existing idea. Triggers include "refine this idea", "what's the core of this", "help me cut this down", "is this idea any good", "challenge this idea", "help me decide if this is worth pursuing", "strip this back", "what's essential here", or when an idea has too many features and needs focus. ([aaronbassett/agent-foundry](https://github.com/aaronbassett/agent-foundry/tree/HEAD/plugins/ideas/skills/refine) · ★4)
- **ideas:riff**: This skill should be used when the user wants sustained divergent exploration of a theme — riffing, branching, finding unexpected angles without converging toward a vision or spec. Works independently, before ideation (to generate raw material), or after refinement (to re-expand a stripped idea). Uses a toolkit of 22 creative techniques adapted to conversational energy. Produces a branching outline of ideas explored. Triggers include "riff on", "explore ideas around", "let's brainstorm about", "what could we do with", "help me think around", "idea jam", "think out loud about", "go wild with",… ([aaronbassett/agent-foundry](https://github.com/aaronbassett/agent-foundry/tree/HEAD/plugins/ideas/skills/riff) · ★4)
- **ideashell**: IdeaShell (闪念贝壳) is the user's personal notes app. They capture ideas, voice recordings and to-dos in it, and it serves as their long-term personal memory. Use this skill to read from and write to their notebook — when they want to find or revisit something they wrote down, recall what someone said in a recording, save a conclusion or idea, manage to-dos, or when the conversation surfaces a stable fact or decision worth remembering. Also use it when the user mentions IdeaShell, 闪念贝壳, my notes, 我的笔记, 录音, 待办, or 灵感. ([RoundRedDot/ideashell-skill](https://github.com/RoundRedDot/ideashell-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/ideashell))
